Constants or Custom Variables from Shopify
var guiloSetup = 12;
var imposSetup = 15;
var stockWaste = 1.05;
var extraArts = 15;
var cuttingBlk = 500;
var cutCost = 11;
var q = {F1.price};

var _a = {art} * {extraArts};
var _a2 = {_a} <= {extraArts} ? 0 : ({_a} - {extraArts});
var boxBoardC = 0.07;

var totalSetupCost = {imposSetup} + {guiloSetup} + {_a2};
var totalLeaveSheets = (({F1.price} * {F4.price}) * {stockWaste}) * {F3.price};
var contentClickCost = {totalLeaveSheets} * {F5.price};
var totalContentCost = ({totalLeaveSheets} * {F6.price}) + {contentClickCost} + ({boxBoardC} * {q});
var cuttingCost = {totalLeaveSheets} / {cuttingBlk} * {cutCost};

var paddingRate = ({q} >= 1 && {q} <= 250) ? .2 : (
    ({q} >= 251 && {q} <= 500) ? 0.2 : (
    ({q} >= 501 && {q} <= 1000) ? 0.15 : (
    ({q} >= 1001 && {q} <= 1500) ? 0.15 : (
    ({q} >= 1501 && {q} <= 2000) ? 0.10 : (
    ({q} >= 2001 && {q} <= 3000) ? 0.10 : 0.10)))));

var paddingCost = {q} * {paddingRate};

var subTotal = {totalSetupCost} + {totalContentCost} + {cuttingCost} + {paddingCost};

var profitMargin = ({subTotal} >= 1 && {subTotal} <= 500) ? 0.8 : (
    ({subTotal} >= 501 && {subTotal} <= 1000) ? 0.8 : (
    ({subTotal} >= 1001 && {subTotal} <= 1500) ? 0.8 : (
    ({subTotal} >= 1501 && {subTotal} <= 2000) ? 0.75 : (
    ({subTotal} >= 2001 && {subTotal} <= 2500) ? 0.72 : (
    ({subTotal} >= 2501 && {subTotal} <= 3000) ? 0.72 : (
    ({subTotal} >= 3001 && {subTotal} <= 4000) ? 0.65 : (
    ({subTotal} >= 4001 && {subTotal} <= 5000) ? 0.55 : (
    ({subTotal} >= 5001 && {subTotal} <= 7500) ? 0.52 : (
    ({subTotal} >= 7501 && {subTotal} <= 10000) ? 0.47 : (
    ({subTotal} >= 10001 && {subTotal} <= 15000) ? 0.42 : (
    ({subTotal} >= 15001 && {subTotal} <= 20000) ? 0.41 : 0)))))))))));

var total2 = ({subTotal} + ({subTotal} * {profitMargin})) * 1.1;

This JSON configuration captures all the complex pricing logic from your notepad printing calculator, including:

1. **Artwork Setup Logic**: First artwork free, additional artworks at $15 each
2. **Tiered Padding Costs**: 7 different quantity tiers for padding rates
3. **Complex Profit Margins**: 13 different profit margin tiers based on subtotal
4. **Double GST Application**: The calculator applies 1.1 twice (once for GST, once as final multiplier)
5. **Stock Waste Factor**: 5% waste factor applied to paper calculations
6. **Box Board Cost**: Additional $0.07 per pad for backing material
7. **Cutting Costs**: Based on total sheets divided by cutting block size
8. **Conditional Field Visibility**: Artworks field only shows when quantity > 1

The calculator handles professional notepad printing with all production variables, setup costs, and sophisticated tiered pricing that scales with both quantity and job value.

Constants or Custom Variables from Shopify
var guiloSetup = 12;
var imposSetup = 15;
var stockWaste = 1.05;
var extraArts = 15;
var cuttingBlk = 500;
var cutCost = 11;
var q = {F1.price};

var _a = {art} * {extraArts};
var _a2 = {_a} <= {extraArts} ? 0 : ({_a} - {extraArts});
var boxBoardC = 0.15;

var totalSetupCost = {imposSetup} + {guiloSetup} + {_a2};
var totalLeaveSheets = (({F1.price} * {F7.price}) * {stockWaste}) * {F8.price};
var contentClickCost = {totalLeaveSheets} * {F10.price};
var totalContentCost = ({totalLeaveSheets} * {F11.price}) + {contentClickCost} + ({boxBoardC} * {q});
var cuttingCost = {totalLeaveSheets} / {cuttingBlk} * {cutCost};

var paddingRate = ({q} >= 1 && {q} <= 250) ? .3 : (
    ({q} >= 251 && {q} <= 500) ? 0.3 : (
    ({q} >= 501 && {q} <= 1000) ? 0.25 : (
    ({q} >= 1001 && {q} <= 1500) ? 0.25 : (
    ({q} >= 1501 && {q} <= 2000) ? 0.20 : (
    ({q} >= 2001 && {q} <= 3000) ? 0.20 : 0.20)))));

var paddingCost = {q} * {paddingRate};

var subTotal = {totalSetupCost} + {totalContentCost} + {cuttingCost} + {paddingCost};

var profitMargin = ({subTotal} >= 1 && {subTotal} <= 500) ? 0.8 : (
    ({subTotal} >= 501 && {subTotal} <= 1000) ? 0.8 : (
    ({subTotal} >= 1001 && {subTotal} <= 1500) ? 0.8 : (
    ({subTotal} >= 1501 && {subTotal} <= 2000) ? 0.75 : (
    ({subTotal} >= 2001 && {subTotal} <= 2500) ? 0.72 : (
    ({subTotal} >= 2501 && {subTotal} <= 3000) ? 0.72 : (
    ({subTotal} >= 3001 && {subTotal} <= 4000) ? 0.65 : (
    ({subTotal} >= 4001 && {subTotal} <= 5000) ? 0.55 : (
    ({subTotal} >= 5001 && {subTotal} <= 7500) ? 0.52 : (
    ({subTotal} >= 7501 && {subTotal} <= 10000) ? 0.47 : (
    ({subTotal} >= 10001 && {subTotal} <= 15000) ? 0.42 : (
    ({subTotal} >= 15001 && {subTotal} <= 20000) ? 0.41 : 0)))))))))));

var total2 = ({subTotal} + ({subTotal} * {profitMargin})) * 1.1;

{total2}*1.1

Constants or Custom Variables from Shopify
var imposSetup = 15;
var guiloSetup = 12;
var extraArts = 15;
var stockWaste = 1.05;
var cuttingBlk = 500;
var cutCost = 11;
var celloSetup = {F2} == 'None' ? 0 : 25;
var _a = {art} * {extraArts};
var _a2 = {_a} <= {extraArts} ? 0 : ({_a} - {extraArts});

var totalSetupCost = {imposSetup} + {guiloSetup} + {_a2} + {celloSetup};
var totalCostOfSheets = ((({F1.price}/{F4.price}) / 1000) * {F5.price}) * {stockWaste};
var totalSheetsPrinted = {F1.Price}/{F4.price} * {stockWaste};
var clickCost = {totalSheetsPrinted} * {F3.price};
var cuttingCost = {totalSheetsPrinted} / {cuttingBlk} * {cutCost};

var celloCost2 = {F2} == 'None' ? 0 : ({totalSheetsPrinted} * {F2.price});

var subTotal = {totalSetupCost} + {totalCostOfSheets} + {clickCost} + {cuttingCost} + {celloCost2};

var profitMargin = ({subTotal} >= 1 && {subTotal} <= 50.999) ? 1.8 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 51 && {subTotal} <= 74.999) ? 1.6 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 75 && {subTotal} <= 100.999) ? 1.45 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 101 && {subTotal} <= 150.999) ? 1.35 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 151 && {subTotal} <= 200.999) ? 1.2 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 201 && {subTotal} <= 300.999) ? 0.83 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 301 && {subTotal} <= 400.999) ? 0.67 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 401 && {subTotal} <= 500.999) ? 0.54 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 501 && {subTotal} <= 1000.999) ? 0.33 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 1001 && {subTotal} <= 5000.999) ? 0.32 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 5001 && {subTotal} <= 100000.999) ? 0.31 : 200))))))))));

var total = ({subTotal} + ({subTotal}*{profitMargin})) * 1.1;

var imposSetup = 15;
var guiloSetup = 12;
var extraArts = 15;
var stockWaste = 1.05;
var cuttingBlk = 500;
var cutCost = 11;
var _a = {art} * {extraArts};
var _a2 = {_a} <= {extraArts} ? 0 : ({_a} - {extraArts});
var totalSetupCost = {imposSetup} + {guiloSetup} + {_a2};
var totalCostOfSheets = ((({F1.price}/{F4.price}) / 1000) * {stockWaste}) * {F5.price};
var totalSheetsPrinted = {F1.Price}/{F4.price} * {stockWaste};
var clickCost = {totalSheetsPrinted} * {F2.price} * {F3.price};
var cuttingCost = {totalSheetsPrinted} / {cuttingBlk} * {cutCost};
var subTotal = {totalSetupCost} + {totalCostOfSheets} + {clickCost} + {cuttingCost};

var profitMargin = ({subTotal} >= 1 && {subTotal} <= 50.999) ? 1.7 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 51 && {subTotal} <= 74.999) ? 1.55 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 75 && {subTotal} <= 100.999) ? 1.35 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 101 && {subTotal} <= 150.999) ? 1.30 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 151 && {subTotal} <= 200.999) ? 1.15 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 201 && {subTotal} <= 300.999) ? 0.70 : (

This JSON configuration captures all the complex pricing logic from your custom flyer printing calculator, including:

1. **Complex 12-Tier Profit Margin System**: Ranging from 170% for small jobs down to 25% for large jobs, with a special fixed $200 margin for jobs over $100k

2. **Artwork Setup Logic**: First artwork included in base setup, additional artworks at $15 each using the conditional formula

3. **Sheet Calculation Logic**: A4 flyers use 2 sheets per unit in the calculation (quantity/2 = total sheets needed)

4. **Print Sides Multiplier**: Single side = 1x click cost, double side = 2x click cost

5. **Paper Stock Options**: Three uncoated bond options (80GSM, 90GSM, 100GSM) with different pricing per 1000 sheets

6. **Double GST Application**: 1.1 * 1.1 = 21% total tax/markup effect

7. **Production Constants**: 5% stock waste, cutting blocks of 500 sheets, setup costs for imposition and guillotine

8. **Comprehensive Quantity Range**: From 50 to 5000 flyers with 14 different quantity options

The calculator handles professional flyer printing with sophisticated pricing that accounts for material costs, production efficiency, artwork setup, and complex margin structures that favor smaller quantity orders while remaining competitive for bulk orders.



With Compliments Slips

Constants or Custom Variables from Shopify

var imposSetup = 15;
var guiloSetup = 12;
var extraArts = 15;
var stockWaste = 1.05;
var cuttingBlk = 500;
var cutCost = 11;
var _a = {art} * {extraArts};
var _a2 = {_a} <= {extraArts} ? 0 : ({_a} - {extraArts});
var totalSetupCost = {imposSetup} + {guiloSetup} + {_a2};
var totalCostOfSheets = ((({F1.price}/{F4.price}) / 1000) * {F5.price}) * {stockWaste};
var totalSheetsPrinted = {F1.Price}/{F4.price} * {stockWaste};
var clickCost = {totalSheetsPrinted} * {F2.price} * {F3.price};
var cuttingCost = {totalSheetsPrinted} / {cuttingBlk} * {cutCost};
var subTotal = {totalSetupCost} + {totalCostOfSheets} + {clickCost} + {cuttingCost};

var profitMargin = ({subTotal} >= 1 && {subTotal} <= 50.999) ? 1.7 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 51 && {subTotal} <= 74.999) ? 1.55 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 75 && {subTotal} <= 100.999) ? 1.35 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 101 && {subTotal} <= 150.999) ? 1.30 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 151 && {subTotal} <= 200.999) ? 1.15 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 201 && {subTotal} <= 300.999) ? 0.70 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 301 && {subTotal} <= 400.999) ? 0.53 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 401 && {subTotal} <= 500.999) ? 0.40 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 501 && {subTotal} <= 1000.999) ? 0.30 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 1001 && {subTotal} <= 5000.999) ? 0.30 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 5001 && {subTotal} <= 100000.999) ? 0.25 : 200))))))))));

var total = ({subTotal} + ({subTotal}*{profitMargin})) * 1.1;

{total}*1.1

2. **Complex 12-Tier Profit Margin System**: Ranging from 170% for small jobs down to 25% for large jobs, with a special fixed $200 margin for jobs over $100k

3. **Artwork Setup Logic**: First artwork included in base setup, additional artworks at $15 each using the conditional formula

4. **Efficient Sheet Calculation**: DL flyers use 6 sheets per unit, making them more cost-effective for material usage

5. **Print Sides Multiplier**: Single side = 1x click cost, double side = 2x click cost

6. **Paper Stock Options**: Three uncoated bond options (80GSM, 90GSM, 100GSM) with different pricing per 1000 sheets

7. **Double GST Application**: 1.1 * 1.1 = 21% total tax/markup effect

8. **Production Constants**: 5% stock waste, cutting blocks of 500 sheets, setup costs for imposition and guillotine

9. **Comprehensive Quantity Range**: From 50 to 5000 flyers with 14 different quantity options

The DL format is particularly efficient for promotional materials, direct mail campaigns, and handouts where compact size and cost-effectiveness are priorities. The calculator reflects this with favorable material usage ratios and competitive pricing for bulk orders.



Saddle Stitch Books


Constants or Custom Variables from Shopify

var guiloSetup = 12;
var imposSetup = 15;
var celloSetup = {F6} == 'None' ? 0 : 25;
var stockWaste = 1.05;
var extraArts = 15;
var cuttingBlk = 500;
var cutCost = 11;
var binderSetup= 30;
var binderPerBook = 0.2;
var binderyLaborperhour = 60;
var bindersheetsperhour = 5000;

var _a = {art} * {extraArts};
var _a2 = {_a} <= {extraArts} ? 0 : ({_a} - {extraArts});

var totalSetupCost = {imposSetup} + {guiloSetup} + {_a2} + {celloSetup} + {binderSetup};


var totalCoverSheetsA3 = {F3} == 'Self Cover' ? 0 : (({f1.price} * {F8.price}) * {stockWaste});
var coverClickCost = {F5.price} * {totalCoverSheetsA3};
var totalCoverCost = ({totalCoverSheetsA3} * {F4.price}) + {coverClickCost};


var totalContentSheets = (({F1.price} * {F7.price}) * {stockWaste}) * {F8.price};
var contentClickCost = {totalContentSheets} * {F10.price};
var totalContentCost = ({totalContentSheets} * {F11.price}) + {contentClickCost};

var totalSheetsPrinted =  {totalContentSheets} + {totalCoverSheetsA3};
var cuttingCost = {totalSheetsPrinted} / {cuttingBlk} * {cutCost};

var celloCost = {F6} == 'None' ? 0 : ({totalCoverSheetsA3} * {F6.price});



var bindRunCost = ((({totalContentSheets} + {totalCoverSheetsA3}) / {bindersheetsperhour}) * {binderyLaborperhour}) + ({F1.price} * {binderPerBook});


var subTotal = {totalSetupCost} + {totalCoverCost} + {totalContentCost} + {cuttingCost} + {celloCost} + {bindRunCost};

var profitMargin = ({subTotal} >= 1 && {subTotal} <= 49.999) ? 1.7 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 50 && {subTotal} <= 99.999) ? 1.54 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 100 && {subTotal} <= 199.999) ? 1.24 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 200 && {subTotal} <= 299.999) ? 1.1 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 300 && {subTotal} <= 499.999) ? 1.04 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 500 && {subTotal} <= 749.999) ? 0.88 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 750 && {subTotal} <= 999.999) ? 0.78 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 1000 && {subTotal} <= 1249.999) ? 0.7 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 1250 && {subTotal} <= 1499.999) ? 0.64 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 1500 && {subTotal} <= 1749.999) ? 0.52 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 1750 && {subTotal} <= 1999.999) ? 0.47 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 2000 && {subTotal} <= 2499.999) ? 0.42 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 2500 && {subTotal} <= 2999.999) ? 0.4 : (
          ({subTotal} >= 3000 && {subTotal} <= 100000) ? 0.37 : .37)))))))))))));


var total = ({subTotal} + ({subTotal} *{profitMargin})) * 1.1;

{total}*1.1

```json
      "12. Calculate subtotal: setup_cost + cover_cost + content_cost + cutting_cost + celloglaze_cost + binding_run_cost",
      "13. Determine profit margin based on subtotal using 14 different tiers",

    "complex_binding_cost": {
      "description": "Binding cost includes both labor time and per-book charges",
      "formula": "((total_sheets / 5000) * 60) + (quantity * 0.2)"
    },
    "14_tier_margin_system": {
      "description": "Sophisticated 14-tier profit margin system ranging from 170% for small jobs down to 37% for large jobs",
      "note": "Margins decrease as job value increases to remain competitive"
    },
    "saddle_stitch_requirements": {
      "description": "Pages must be in multiples of 4 for proper saddle stitching",
      "page_options": "4pp to 64pp in 4-page increments"
    }
  },
  "key_calculation_variables": {
    "cover_sheet_calculation": "quantity * finish_size_multiplier * stock_waste (if Hard Cover)",
    "content_sheet_calculation": "quantity * printed_pages_value * stock_waste * finish_size_multiplier",
    "waste_factor": "5% waste applied to all sheet calculations",
    "binding_efficiency": "5000 sheets per hour binding capacity",
    "cutting_efficiency": "500 sheets per cutting block"
  }
}
```

This comprehensive JSON configuration captures all the complex pricing logic from your saddle stitch booklet calculator, including:

1. **14-Tier Profit Margin System**: Sophisticated margin structure ranging from 170% for small jobs down to 37% for large jobs

2. **Conditional Cover Logic**: Self Cover option eliminates separate cover sheet calculations

3. **Complex Binding Calculations**: Includes both labor time (based on sheets per hour) and per-book charges

4. **Artwork Setup Logic**: First artwork included, additional at $15 each

5. **Conditional Celloglaze Setup**: Additional $25 setup only when celloglaze is selected

6. **Comprehensive Stock Options**: Multiple satin and uncoated options for both cover and content

7. **Page Count Validation**: 16 different page options from 4pp to 64pp in 4-page increments (saddle stitch requirement)

8. **Size Multipliers**: Different finish sizes affect material calculations

9. **Dual Print Types**: Separate print type options for cover and content pages

10. **Production Constants**: Includes all setup costs, waste factors, cutting costs, and binding parameters

The calculator handles professional saddle stitch booklet production with sophisticated pricing that accounts for binding complexity, material efficiency, optional protective coatings, and tiered margins that scale appropriately with job size and value.

var guiloSetup = 12;
var imposSetup = 15;
var stockWaste = 1.05;

var cuttingBlk = 500;
var cutCost = 11;

var wirebindperbook = 1.16;
var binderyLaborperhour = 70;
var punchsheetsperhour = 15000;
var celloSetup = ({F6} == 'None' && {F10} == 'None') ? 0 : 25;

var _a = {art} * {extraArts};
var _a2 = {_a} <= {extraArts} ? 0 : ({_a} - {extraArts});

var outFront = ({F3.price} * {F1});
var totalFrontCoverSheets = ({F4} == 'None' ? 0 : (({F1} / {F14.price}) * {stockWaste}));
var coverClickCost = ({F5.price} * {totalFrontCoverSheets});
var totalFrontCoverCost = ({totalFrontCoverSheets} * {F4.price}) + {coverClickCost};
var FrontcelloCost = ({F6} == 'None' ? 0 : ({totalFrontCoverSheets} * {F6.price}));

var outBack = ({F7.price} * {F1});
var totalBackCoverSheets = ({F8} == 'None' ? 0 : (({F1} / {F14.price}) * {stockWaste}));
var coverClickCostBack = ({F9.price} * {totalBackCoverSheets});
var totalBackCoverCost = ({totalBackCoverSheets} * {F4.price}) + {coverClickCostBack};
var BackcelloCost = ({F10} == 'None' ? 0 : ({totalBackCoverSheets} * {F10.price}));

var totalContentSheets = ((({F1} * {F11}) / 2) / {F14.price}) * {stockWaste};
var contentClickCost = ({totalContentSheets} * {F13.price});
var totalContentCost = ({totalContentSheets} * {F12.price}) + {contentClickCost};

var totalPrintCost = {totalFrontCoverCost} + {totalBackCoverCost} + {FrontcelloCost} + {BackcelloCost} + {outFront} + {outBack} + {totalContentCost};
var totalSetupCosts = {guiloSetup} + {imposSetup} + {punchSetup} + {celloSetup} + {_a2};

var bookSheets = {F11} / 2;
var contentSheetThickness = ({F12} == 'Satin 128GSM') ? 0.12 : (
    ({F12} == 'Satin 150GSM') ? 0.135 : (
    ({F12} == 'Uncoated Bond 80GSM') ? 0.1 : (
    ({F12} == 'Uncoated Bond 90GSM') ? 0.11 : (
    ({F12} == 'Uncoated Bond 140GSM') ? 0.2 : (
    ({F12} == 'Uncoated Bond 100GSM') ? 0.125 : (
    ({F12} == 'Satin 300GSM') ? 0.4 : (
    0)))))));
var bookThickness = {bookSheets} * {contentSheetThickness};

var pricePerRing = ({bookThickness} <= 8) ? 0.13065 : (
    ({bookThickness} <= 10) ? 0.157 : (
    ({bookThickness} <= 12) ? 0.2242 : (
    ({bookThickness} <= 14) ? 0.25 : (
    ({bookThickness} <= 16) ? 0.2895 : (
    ({bookThickness} <= 18) ? 0.321 : (
    ({bookThickness} <= 20) ? 0.4141 : (
    ({bookThickness} <= 22) ? 0.516 : (
    ({bookThickness} <= 24) ? 0.563 : (
    ({bookThickness} <= 28) ? 0.6392 : (
    ({bookThickness} <= 31) ? 0.7172 : (
    ({bookThickness} <= 33) ? 0.7558 : (
    ({bookThickness} <= 35) ? 0.829 : (
    ({bookThickness} <= 38) ? 0.9042 : (
    ({bookThickness} <= 41) ? 1.201 : (
    ({bookThickness} <= 48) ? 1.248 : (
    ({bookThickness} <= 53) ? 1.248 : 1.248))))))))))))))));

var priceofwire = ({F14} == 'A6 Portrait' || {F14} == 'A6 Landscape' || {F14} == 'DL Landscape' || {F14} == 'A5 Landscape') ? 
    ({pricePerRing} * {F1}) / 2 : 
    ({pricePerRing} * {F1});


var baseValue = ({F1} * {F11}) / 2;
var additionalF8 = ({F8} == 'None' ? 0 : {F1});
var additionalF4 = ({F4} == 'None' ? 0 : {F1});
var totalPunch = {baseValue} + {additionalF8} + {additionalF4};
var sheetsToPunch = {totalPunch} * {stockWaste};

var punchPrice = ({sheetsToPunch} / {punchsheetsperhour})* {binderyLaborperhour};

var cuttingCost = (({totalContentSheets} + {totalFrontCoverSheets} + {totalBackCoverSheets}) / {cuttingBlk}) * {cutCost};

var BizCost = {totalPrintCost} + {totalSetupCosts} + {priceofwire} + {punchPrice} + {cuttingCost} + ({F1} * {wirebindperbook});


var profitMargin = ({BizCost} >= 1 && {BizCost} <= 500) ? 0.9 : (
    ({BizCost} > 500 && {BizCost} <= 1000) ? 0.9 : (
    ({BizCost} > 1000 && {BizCost} <= 1500) ? 0.80 : (
    ({BizCost} > 1500 && {BizCost} <= 2000) ? 0.75 : (
    ({BizCost} > 2000 && {BizCost} <= 2500) ? 0.70 : (
    ({BizCost} > 2500 && {BizCost} <= 3000) ? 0.67 : (
    ({BizCost} > 3000 && {BizCost} <= 4000) ? 0.65 : (
    ({BizCost} > 4000 && {BizCost} <= 5000) ? 0.55 : (
    ({BizCost} > 5000 && {BizCost} <= 7500) ? 0.52 : (
    ({BizCost} > 7500 && {BizCost} <= 10000) ? 0.47 : (
    ({BizCost} > 10000 && {BizCost} <= 15000) ? 0.42 : (
    ({BizCost} > 15000 && {BizCost} <= 100000) ? 0.41 : 0)))))))))));

var subTotal = ({BizCost} + ({BizCost}*{profitMargin}));

var total = {subTotal} * 1.15;
{total} + 44

var taxRateOnCustom = {f6} == 'Custom' ? 1.1 : 1;

var subTotal = (((({sqm} * ({tier}+{_sidesCost})))* {taxRateOnCustom})+ ({f2.price} * {q})+ {_a2}) * 0.95;




var total = {subTotal} < 129 ? 129 : ({subTotal});

Else: {F7} >= 1800 	Price = {total} + 45
Else: {F3} >= 1800 	Price = {total} + 45
Else: {f6} == '1200mm x 2400mm'  Price = {total} + 45
Else: {total}>=129  Price = {total} * 1.1

This comprehensive JSON configuration captures all the complex pricing logic from your Premium Pull Up Banner calculator, including:

1. **Complex Quantity-Based Pricing**: 26 different pricing tiers for each size option, with rates decreasing as quantity increases

This comprehensive JSON configuration captures all the complex pricing logic from your Custom Poster Printing calculator, including:

1. **Sophisticated Square Meter Pricing**: 51 different pricing tiers for each paper type based on total square meters
2. **Material-Specific Rate Tables**: Separate pricing structures for 250GSM Satin vs 200GSM Yuppo synthetic paper
3. **Custom Size Premium**: Additional 10% tax applied to custom dimensions
4. **Conditional Field Visibility**: Width/length fields only show for custom sizes, artwork field only for single quantities
5. **Minimum Order Handling**: $117 minimum order value plus $15 handling fee applied to all orders
6. **Artwork Pricing Logic**: First artwork included, additional at $5 each
7. **Standard A-Series Sizes**: Pre-defined dimensions for A2, A1, and A0 formats
8. **Complex Calculation Flow**: Square meter calculation, rate determination, custom tax, GST, and minimum order enforcement

The calculator handles professional large-format poster printing with economies of scale that reward bulk orders through progressively lower per-square-meter rates, while maintaining premium pricing for small orders through the minimum order value system.

  "calculation_formula": {
    "description": "Exact WooCommerce Folded Printed Flyers formula implementation",
    "steps": [
      "1. Calculate celloglaze setup cost: IF celloglaze in ['1 Side Gloss', '2 Side Gloss', '1 Side Matt', '2 Side Matt'] THEN 16 ELSE 0",
      "2. Calculate artwork setup cost: IF artworks > 1 THEN (artworks * extra_arts) - extra_arts ELSE 0",
      "3. Calculate total setup cost: impos_setup + guilo_setup + artwork_setup + folder_setup",
      "4. Calculate sheets needed: (quantity / items_per_sheet) * stock_waste",
      "5. Calculate stock cost: (sheets_needed / 1000) * paper_stock.price",
      "6. Calculate click cost: sheets_needed * print_sides.price * print_type.price",
      "7. Calculate cutting cost: (sheets_needed / cutting_block) * cut_cost",
      "8. Calculate folding cost: (quantity * fold_type.price / 1000) * fold_per_thousand",
      "9. Calculate cello cost: IF celloglaze != 'None' THEN sheets_needed * celloglaze.price ELSE 0",
      "10. Calculate BizCost: setup + stock + clicks + cutting + folding + cello_setup + cello_cost",
      "11. Determine profit margin based on finish_size AND quantity threshold (4000) AND BizCost value",
      "12. Calculate Total: (BizCost + (BizCost * profit_margin)) * GST_rate",
      "13. Round final price to nearest cent"
    ],
    "key_differences_from_business_cards": {
      "folding_cost": "Additional folding operation with setup and per-thousand costs",

      "3. Calculate total setup cost: impos_setup + guilo_setup + cello_setup (no artwork setup in main setup)",
      "4. Calculate sheets needed: (quantity / cards_per_sheet) * stock_waste",
      "5. Calculate stock cost: (sheets_needed / 1000) * paper_stock.price",
      "6. Calculate click cost: sheets_needed * print_sides.price * print_type.price",
      "7. Calculate cutting cost: (sheets_needed / cutting_block) * cut_cost",
      "8. Calculate cello cost: IF celloglaze != 'None' THEN sheets_needed * celloglaze.price ELSE 0",
      "9. Calculate BizCost (business cost): setup + stock + clicks + cutting + cello",
      "10. Determine profit margin tier based on celloglaze selection AND BizCost value",
      "11. Calculate SubTotal: BizCost + artwork_setup_cost + (BizCost * profit_margin)",
      "12. Calculate Total: SubTotal * GST_rate",
      "13. Apply final GST multiplication: Total * 1.1 (double GST application)",
      "14. Round final price to nearest cent"

      "6. Calculate cutting cost: (sheets_needed / cutting_block) * cut_cost",
      "7. Calculate BizCost (business cost): setup + stock + clicks + cutting",
      "8. Determine profit margin tier based on BizCost value",
      "9. Calculate SubTotal: BizCost + (BizCost * profit_margin)",
      "10. Calculate Total: SubTotal * GST_rate",
      "11. Round final price to nearest cent"
    ],
    "key_differences_from_perfect_bound": {
      "artwork_setup": "Multiple artworks incur additional setup costs ($15 per extra artwork)",
      "no_binding": "No binding costs - just cutting to finished size",
      "cards_per_sheet": "Uses cards-per-sheet calculation (21 cards per sheet for 90x55mm)",
      "profit_margins": "Different margin structure optimized for small format printing",
      "simpler_structure": "No cover/content separation - single stock type throughout"
    }
  },
  "validation_rules": {
    "min_quantity": 250,
